# F-family aggregate — ELAI deep feature discovery

## Document contract

- **Family:** F (VibeSec/SHARD wiring-enforcement sessions F-1..F-10 plus post-F extensions F-2c, F-11, F-12, F-13; doctrine F-0 / wiring-doctrine).
- **Inputs:** `DEEP-F-001.discovery.md`, `DEEP-F-002.discovery.md`, `06-F-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md`.
- **Policy:** Every distinct atomic record is preserved with its ID and source anchor (ledger file + line). No prose compression. No silent drop of minor atoms.
- **Namespacing:** Deep IDs keep native form under ledger prefix (`F-001/DF001-…`, `F-002/DF002-…`). Summary IDs are `SUMMARY/<FG|F1|…>`.
- **Cross-file interactions / summary deltas / ambiguity-conflict:** kept under ledger namespaces (e.g. `F-001/DF001-X-I003`, `F-002/DF002-SD-015`, `F-001/DF001-F4-C001`).
- **NEWER-PLAN PRECEDENCE (within family):** An explicit SUPERSEDED/replacement/as-built reconciliation in a deep source beats the summary and older plan prose; otherwise a higher plan number is newer and wins **target state**. Losing descriptions are retained as superseded notes with anchors.
- **Status claims:** Plan language (`PLANNED`, `shipped`, `as-built`) remains a source assertion. Deep passes did not independently verify code.

### Unit `F-8` (31 feature records)

- **F-001/DF001-F8-E001** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=134) F-8 adds an LLM-free rule-based pre-admission detector for config/structural-change conflicts against locked keys, parameter bounds, and active tripwires. _(anchor: L1-L7; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E002** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=135) It emits Contradictory, ResourceContention, or CausalViolation and uses WARN-then-escalate on self-modification only. _(anchor: L3, L9-L15; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E003** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=136) The source treats SCF’s 27.9% precision as simulation-only and unvalidated outside its synthetic scaffold, so it is not an ELAI target or hard-block basis. _(anchor: L7-L12; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E004** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=137) The inter-agent BSP half is RunPod-pending and out of scope because parallel cap-3 false positives become noise; Graphiti’s cloud-lane contradiction judge is a future alternative. _(anchor: L14-L15; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E005** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=138) The self-mod stack and apply path are asserted shipped, while C-50 ContractSpec is asserted absent; the new names avoid a future precondition/postcondition namespace collision. _(anchor: L5, L17-L29; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E006** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=139) Existing name-based locked-key checks, numeric promotion/consensus/tripwire gates, and interface set comparison do not answer semantic postcondition conflict. _(anchor: L11-L13, L19-L27; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E007** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=140) F-8 is a pre-gate before two_gate_promote and before ConfigArchive admission; it augments rather than replaces the name-match safety gate. _(anchor: L19-L23; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E008** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=141) ConflictKind has three arms and SymbolicIntent stores asserts and requires vectors of IntentCondition. _(anchor: L49-L54; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E009** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=142) IntentCondition reuses ParameterKey and supports Equals, GreaterThan, LessThan, Locked, Unlocked, and TripwireQuiescent predicates. _(anchor: L53-L55; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E010** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=143) ProcessModel snapshots locked keys, active TripwireKind values, and current numeric bounds. _(anchor: L54-L56; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E011** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=144) detect_conflicts is pure, deterministic, returns all conflicts, and produces SymbolicConflict kind/key/detail records. _(anchor: L55-L57; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E012** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=145) Two adapters translate StructuralChange and ConfigVariant into symbolic intents; conflict_fingerprint produces a BLAKE3 prefix; production code has no unwrap/expect or unnamed thresholds. _(anchor: L57-L59; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E013** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=146) On any conflict the system emits one symbolic_conflict_detected row per conflict. Interactive mode requires operator confirmation; autonomous mode holds pending and escalates. _(anchor: L61-L63; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E014** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=147) A detected conflict cannot be overridden by prompt/candidate content and never silently applies; only an explicit operator-confirm row admits it. _(anchor: L62-L63, L112-L118; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E015** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=148) SymbolicConflictPayload carries fingerprint, kind, key, detail, candidate_id, and fsm_substate in one schema-baseline-plus-one action. _(anchor: L65; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E016** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=149) symbolic_conflicts returns SymbolicConflictRow with seq, time, fingerprint, kind, key, detail, and candidate_id for dashboard consumption. _(anchor: L67; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E017** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=150) get_symbolic_conflicts has feature-on and empty-array feature-off twins; ReasoningDrawer renders per-kind tone badges and the TS stub/invoke handler complete the frontend pair. _(anchor: L69; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E018** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=151) current_process_model and tripwire_quiescent_violated implement the ELAI-specific rule that increased self-mod activity violates an active tripwire’s quiescence requirement. _(anchor: L71; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E019** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=152) The decision document must capture terminal WARN/escalate policy, BSP deferment, collision-safe names, rule definitions, grounding status, and fully in-process tiering. _(anchor: L73; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E020** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=153) The produced-symbol contract enumerates detector types/adapters/helpers, viewer query/row, SSB action/payload, and dashboard command. _(anchor: L75-L94; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E021** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=154) The consumed-symbol contract names live apply/safety/type/tripwire/viewer/dashboard anchors and corrects observe_post_consensus_tripwires to agent_pool.rs. _(anchor: L95-L105; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E022** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=155) A later as-built reachability reconciliation routes CLI run_apply through screen_on_runtime and screen_config_variant_for_conflicts, with screen_config_variant_for_conflicts emitting the event before admission. _(anchor: L107-L110; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E023** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=156) The live trigger applies contradictory_locked_variant.json through CLI or dashboard, expects a pending-not-applied candidate, a matching BLAKE3-prefix row, and ReasoningDrawer rendering. _(anchor: L125-L128; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E024** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=157) Deterministic checks verify core symbols, live admission wiring, five SSB sites, viewer, frontend and TS binding, drift, default-on/no bypass, and the decision record. _(anchor: L130-L162; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E025** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=158) Acceptance requires all three conflict fixtures plus a clean case, operator-confirm semantics, no C-50 names, no BSP scope, live dashboard display, golden/schema parity, cargo/auditor green, and no new dependency. _(anchor: L164-L177; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E026** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=159) Tests cover each conflict kind, clean behavior, stable fingerprint, real apply/hold behavior, SSB round-trip, and an end-to-end temp-database smoke through the production path. _(anchor: L179-L184; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E027** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=160) The declared artifacts include symbolic_conflict.rs, four fixtures, three contract suites, an observable smoke, decision doc, apply/SSB/viewer/module edits, and complete dashboard wiring. _(anchor: L186-L204; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E028** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=161) Before any tightening to hard block, the decision record must measure false-positive rate on at least 20 real self-mod proposals. _(anchor: L215-L217; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E029** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=162) Process-model staleness is mitigated by reading the latest tripwire outcome inside admission; a smoke test must prove freshness. _(anchor: L218; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E030** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=163) Semantic conflict accuracy remains a design hypothesis; SHARD semantic performance underperformed syntactic performance and no ELAI catch-rate may be claimed before measurement. _(anchor: L220-L221; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F8-E031** `[E]` (unit=`F-8`, ledger-line=164) F-7 can later supply a proven locked-key set, while F-10 composes as a separate CWE-class monotonicity axis. _(anchor: L238-L240; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_

### Unit `F-9` (30 feature records)

- **F-001/DF001-F9-E001** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=170) F-9 extends a real PyPI/npm existence-and-age checker from PR time to Edit/Write PreToolUse time, adds crates.io, per-ecosystem thresholds, a local cache, live block telemetry, CLI, and dashboard. _(anchor: L1-L5; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E002** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=171) D-11 is explicitly PLANNED/not shipped and is a hard blocker; E-41/E-11 are shipped composition surfaces and B-19 latency budgets are planned. _(anchor: L7-L13; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E003** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=172) The source corrects “shape-only” to a real HTTP checker and narrows the true gaps to invocation timing, cargo coverage, and a hard-coded 730-day age threshold. _(anchor: L17-L25; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E004** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=173) Network lookup is explicitly not a fail-closed hot-path guarantee: cache hits are in-process; misses are Tier-2 best effort and may fail open under registry unavailability. _(anchor: L27-L29; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E005** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=174) Socket Firewall is defense in depth at install time but is moved to an operations runbook and contributes no plan code. _(anchor: L29; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E006** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=175) Existing E-41 dep parsing is a cheap deterministic PR prefilter and lacks Cargo section awareness; F-9 preserves it while adding registry-grounded write-time enforcement. _(anchor: L31-L39; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E007** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=176) _check_cargo calls crates.io through the existing HTTP helper, derives age from updated_at/newest created_at, keeps the existing tuple shape, accepts ecosystem in stdin JSON, and maps network failure to inapplicable. _(anchor: L54-L57; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E008** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=177) Default maximum ages become PyPI 730, npm 730, and cargo 1825 days; an explicit claim overrides the default, and 1825 is a tunable unverified design choice. _(anchor: L58; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E009** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=178) Cargo parsing becomes section-aware for dependencies, dev-dependencies, and build-dependencies and returns DepCandidate{name, Ecosystem} for Pypi/Npm/Cargo while keeping the PR leg compatible. _(anchor: L60; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E010** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=179) DepWriteTimeGate fires only for Edit/Write dependency content/path patterns and returns immediately with no finding on other writes. _(anchor: L62-L64; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E011** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=180) LruExistenceCache is a bounded HashMap plus VecDeque with about a one-hour TTL and no new lru dependency. _(anchor: L64-L66; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E012** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=181) A known-good hit allows without network; a miss invokes RegistryCheck; NotFound or TooNew blocks and emits write_time_slopsquat_blocked; Ok populates the cache. _(anchor: L64-L65; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E013** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=182) RegistryUnreachable allows the write but must emit an advisory row; it may never silently pass. _(anchor: L65, L109-L115; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E014** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=183) The gate registers in build_production_hook_dispatcher/dispatch_pre_tool_use and is default-on; ELAI_DEP_WRITETIME_GATE_DISABLE=1 is the only permitted operator kill switch. _(anchor: L68, L98-L108; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E015** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=184) WriteTimeSlopsquatBlockedPayload stores a BLAKE3 dep-name hash, ecosystem, NotFound/TooNew/RegistryUnreachable kind, optional registry age, and timestamp in one baseline-plus-one action. _(anchor: L70; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E016** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=185) write_time_slop_status aggregates recent rows by ecosystem and verdict; DepsSecurityPanel shows daily block/advisory counts and registers a DrawerKey, Tauri DTO, and TS stub. _(anchor: L72; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E017** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=186) The decision document must name cache capacity, one-hour TTL, fail-open/advisory semantics, cargo threshold uncertainty, Socket deferral, real-checker correction, and E-41 composition. _(anchor: L74; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E018** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=187) The explicit tier split says known-good allows and known-bad blocks on cache hit at T1; cache-miss subprocess is the separately gated, fail-open-with-advisory Tier 2. _(anchor: L76-L78; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E019** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=188) The reconciled produced contract adds RegistryExistenceChecker, StubRegistryChecker, SubprocessRegistryChecker, RegistryVerdict, an agnostic extractor, dep_name_blake3_u64, a dedicated dep-check CLI action, dashboard command, and drawer. _(anchor: L80-L108; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E020** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=189) The real hook is bootstrapped from CLI task and dashboard paths; elai dep-check <path> [--offline] reuses analyze_collect and writes the same event. _(anchor: L99-L108; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E021** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=190) The live fixture uses a Rust use of reqquests, expects a NotFound/cargo row within two seconds, a BLAKE3 prefix, a BLOCK line, and refusal before disk. _(anchor: L119-L122; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E022** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=191) Deterministic checks cover wiring trigger, produced types, Pattern-2 event, hook registration, disable-only default, frontend/drift, BLAKE3, and decision doc. _(anchor: L124-L159; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E023** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=192) Acceptance separates a fixed-not_found real-subprocess fixture from a network-gated 50/50 confirmed-404 and zero-false-positive-on-20-real-deps live run. _(anchor: L161-L170; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E024** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=193) Acceptance also requires Cargo extraction/checking, event round-trip/schema, no new dependency, and backward-compatible E-41 PR contracts. _(anchor: L168-L171; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E025** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=194) Tests cover non-dependency no-op, no-network cache hit, real subprocess block, unreachable advisory plus allow, prompt bypass resistance, event round-trip, Cargo parsing, mock-clock threshold, and dashboard DTO parity. _(anchor: L188-L194; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E026** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=195) The declared change set spans the checker, cargo-aware parser, new hook/cache, hook bootstrap, SSB, CLI, two fixtures, two contracts, decisions, drawer/Tauri/stub, and golden lock. _(anchor: L196-L214; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E027** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=196) The 1825-day floor must be validated empirically and remains explicitly unverified. _(anchor: L226-L228; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E028** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=197) False-fire mitigation restricts Cargo extraction to dependency sections; cache-miss hangs use the checker’s ten-second timeout then fail open with an advisory. _(anchor: L229-L231; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F9-E029** `[E]` (unit=`F-9`, ledger-line=198) F-9 must not reimplement D-11, add an LRU crate, or silently unify with F-1; co-sequencing must decide whether to emit both event names or merge under one schema bump. _(anchor: L232-L234; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_

### Unit `F-10` (34 feature records)

- **F-001/DF001-F10-E001** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=205) F-10 fingerprints each generated delta by CWE class and blocks/escalates any class present at iteration N but absent at N-1 across REFINE, auto-advance, and self-modification. _(anchor: L1-L5; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E002** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=206) It is a two-session plan: Phase 1 builds/calibrates the classifier and Phase 2 wires the gate as post-write async auditor evidence rather than a per-write blocking hook. _(anchor: L3-L7, L14-L15; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E003** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=207) B-39, B-22/C-9, D-4, and E-7 are asserted shipped prerequisites; the CWE rule data is a new in-repo dependency surface but no crate may be added without confirmation. _(anchor: L5-L6; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E004** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=208) The source motivates the invariant with externally measured +37.6% critical vulnerabilities over five iterations and approximately 11x LLM-versus-human vulnerability injection, while forbidding their use as ELAI targets. _(anchor: L9-L15, L201-L207; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E005** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=209) Existing correctness/score gates are explicitly blind to code-delta security class, and no current classifier, comparison gate, tripwire kind, autonomy axis, or promotion precondition is asserted in the initial-state section. _(anchor: L17-L25; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E006** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=210) Planned classify_delta returns a deterministically ordered BTreeSet of CweClass for a CodeDelta and Language. _(anchor: L42-L47; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E007** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=211) Planned classes include CWE-78, 95, 502, and 22 plus SQL injection, XSS, weak cryptography, and missing authorization; patterns cover Rust, Python, and TypeScript with confidence metadata. _(anchor: L46-L47; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E008** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=212) Planned implementation is in-process GREP/AST patterning, uses BLAKE3, forbids production unwrap, and does not claim an exhaustive CWE taxonomy. _(anchor: L46-L49; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E009** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=213) Calibration uses 50 known-clean historical patches per language and requires false-positive rate below 5% separately for Rust, Python, and TypeScript. _(anchor: L48-L49; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E010** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=214) If any language misses the threshold, patterns are tightened and remeasured; after three failed tightening passes the workflow escalates rather than spirals. _(anchor: L48; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E011** `[E]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=215) SecurityMonotonicityGate set-diffs prior versus current classes and returns Monotone or Regressed{introduced, prior}; state is keyed by turn_id, step_id, or variant_id. _(anchor: L51-L53; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E012** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=216) Classifier errors become UNKNOWN and escalate to the auditor rather than silently becoming Monotone. _(anchor: L92-L99; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E013** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=217) Planned REFINE wiring consumes the event stream after candidate commit, schedules a background task, emits a block row, and writes workflow/sessions/{id}/security-monotonicity.log without synchronously stalling the pipeline. _(anchor: L54; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E014** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=218) Planned auto-advance wiring adds a security evidence axis whose regression verdict prevents phase advancement. _(anchor: L55; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_
- **F-001/DF001-F10-E015** `[E]` (unit=`F-10`, ledger-line=219) Planned self-mod wiring checks the archive baseline before tau-margin and blocks a variant introducing a new CWE class. _(anchor: L56; source: DEEP-F-001.discovery.md)_

### Unit `F-3` (44 feature records)

- **F-002/DF002-F3-001** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=186) Tier-1 adds per-task least privilege on top of the existing per-FSM-state category gate for researcher/web/MCP agents. _(anchor: F3:L1-L5, L9-L15; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-002** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=187) The task’s minimal tool subset is committed and frozen before the first untrusted repo/web/skill/MCP byte is observed. _(anchor: F3:L3, L11-L13; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-003** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=188) Existing `ToolCategory::ALL` contains 16 categories and `FsmState::check_tool_allowed` provides the category gate. _(anchor: F3:L19-L20; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-004** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=189) The asserted pre-observation boundary is `WebResearchOrchestrator::formulate_query`; `fetch_and_extract` is the untrusted-observation boundary. _(anchor: F3:L13, L22; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-005** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=190) `CommittedToolSet` stores `task_id`, a `BTreeSet<ToolCategory>`, `committed_at_ms`, `frozen`, and `commit_hash`. _(anchor: F3:L51-L53; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-006** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=191) `commit_minimal_set` intersects requested categories with `ToolCategory::ALL`, freezes the result, sorts it, and BLAKE3-hashes the allow-set plus task ID. _(anchor: F3:L52-L54; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-007** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=192) Researcher commitment includes search, read-only fetch, and role-allowed grounding methods, but excludes filesystem write, Bash, and edit. _(anchor: F3:L58; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-008** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=193) `check_against_commitment` classifies each tool and denies an out-of-set tool when the commitment is frozen. _(anchor: F3:L54; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-009** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=194) A frozen set treats an unknown tool as denied, never as default-allowed. _(anchor: F3:L60, L113; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-010** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=195) Enforcement is pure set membership and cannot consult an LLM/model to override a denial. _(anchor: F3:L56, L111; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-011** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=196) `UntrustedTaint::{Clean,Tainted{source,span_hash}}` represents classified untrusted spans. _(anchor: F3:L55; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-012** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=197) `taint_from_verdict` maps E-37 `ClassifierVerdict` into the taint marker. _(anchor: F3:L55-L56; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-013** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=198) A frozen commitment plus tainted span plus out-of-set call emits `untrusted_var_quarantined`. _(anchor: F3:L55, L60; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-014** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=199) `formulate_query` persists the commitment on the orchestrator and emits `tool_filter_committed` through `emit_stage`. _(anchor: F3:L58; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-015** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=200) Both `Fsm::check_tool_allowed` and `mcp::gate::check_tool_allowed` consult the commitment after their existing state/category checks pass. _(anchor: F3:L60; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-016** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=201) An out-of-set attempt reuses existing `tool_denied` and adds the commitment hash to that denial payload. _(anchor: F3:L60-L62; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-017** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=202) New SSB state is one `ToolFilterEvent` variant with `ToolFilterEventKind::{Committed,UntrustedQuarantined}`. _(anchor: F3:L62; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-018** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=203) Its two SQL action spellings are `tool_filter_committed` and `untrusted_var_quarantined`. _(anchor: F3:L62; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-019** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=204) `ToolFilterPayload` carries task ID, BLAKE3 commitment hash, allowed categories, optional denied tool, and optional taint source. _(anchor: F3:L62; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-020** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=205) `capability_flow_blocked` is reserved for full CaMeL and must be absent until it has a producer. _(anchor: F3:L62, L134-L135, L170; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-021** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=206) The schema impact is one Pattern-2 variant with a baseline-plus-one bump and golden round-trip. _(anchor: F3:L62, L171; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-022** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=207) `elai safety tool-filter --fixture …` is a production CLI action that runs the real researcher commitment/enforcement path. _(anchor: F3:L64, L104, L118; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-023** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=208) The fixture injects a request to call `fs.write`; the attempt must be denied after commitment. _(anchor: F3:L64, L118-L120; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-024** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=209) CLI output is JSON containing commitment hash, denied tool, and fixture-suite ASR. _(anchor: F3:L64, L120; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-025** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=210) The deterministic suite contains at least 20 AgentDojo-style web-page injections, each attempting out-of-set escalation. _(anchor: F3:L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-026** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=211) Fixture ASR is calculated before without a commitment and after with a commitment; no AgentDojo number is transferred as an ELAI result. _(anchor: F3:L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-027** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=212) Tier-1 completion requires `asr_after < asr_before` and denial of all 20 injected escalations. _(anchor: F3:L166; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-028** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=213) A real sidecar/cloud researcher ASR run is a separate gated checkbox and not required for Tier-1 done. _(anchor: F3:L68, L167; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-029** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=214) The existing `ToolGateInspector.svelte` gains a Tool Filter/Data-plane section; `commands_c35.rs` maps both new event spellings into extended `ToolGateRowDto`. _(anchor: F3:L66, L114-L116; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-030** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=215) The surface displays allow-set, commitment hash, quarantines, and derived before/after ASR. _(anchor: F3:L66, L115; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-031** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=216) Tool Filter must be default-on; any `ELAI_CAMEL_TOOLFILTER_ENABLE`-named control is specified as disable-only despite its name. _(anchor: F3:L112, L164, L220; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-032** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=217) The live trigger must create commitment, quarantine, and reused denial rows in one run and keep the FSM from advancing with the escalated tool. _(anchor: F3:L117-L120, L163-L170; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-033** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=218) The JSON artifact must contain a 64-hex commitment, `denied_tool="fs.write"`, and strictly lower after-ASR. _(anchor: F3:L120; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-034** `[E/G]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=219) Completion requires executed wiring check, default-on proof, dashboard E2E, schema/golden lock, cross-language parity, workspace compile, and auditor pass. _(anchor: F3:L122-L172; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-035** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=220) Contract tests cover deterministic freezing/hash, out-of-set denial, classifier-to-taint mapping, quarantine emission, both Kind round trips, and reserved-event absence. _(anchor: F3:L175-L179; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-036** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=221) Full CaMeL uses typed `SymbolicVar` values and a non-LLM controller so untrusted-derived data cannot alter control flow. _(anchor: F3:L72-L74; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-037** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=222) Full CaMeL requires an IPC schema break because the researcher returns typed symbolic values rather than raw strings. _(anchor: F3:L74; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-038** `[E/D]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=223) Full CaMeL may be scheduled only after the primary PDF overhead is read, real ELAI IPC overhead preserves the 32K ceiling, and the breaking change is accepted. _(anchor: F3:L70, L74; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-039** `[E/D]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=224) The secondary `2.82x` input/`2.73x` output overhead must not be represented as primary or as measured on ELAI; 4–5x on ELAI is only a likelihood claim. _(anchor: F3:L3-L4, L70, L199-L200, L210; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-040** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=225) A fetch path that bypasses `formulate_query` would miss commitment; the contract must establish no such production path. _(anchor: F3:L208; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-041** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=226) An over-narrow set can deny legitimate research; the mitigation is role-derived grounding capabilities and measurement of false-deny rate on real runs. _(anchor: F3:L209; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-042** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=227) The implementation may use only `BTreeSet`/standard collections and existing BLAKE3; no new dependency or gate-time network/subprocess/RunPod work is allowed. _(anchor: F3:L5, L49, L56, L212-L213; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-043** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=228) The source corrects a nonexistent `crates/plugins/src/prompt_injection.rs` citation to `crates/safety/src/prompt_armor.rs` plus `spotlight.rs`. _(anchor: F3:L26, L204; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F3-044** `[E/D]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-3`, ledger-line=229) AgentDojo’s `57.7%→6.84% at 73% utility` and CaMeL’s 77% provable-security figure are external ceilings, not ELAI measurements. _(anchor: F3:L13, L199-L200; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

### Unit `F-5` (42 feature records)

- **F-002/DF002-F5-001** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=237) F-5 extends existing memory bi-temporal decay with a cross-subsystem invalidate→demote→broadcast cascade rather than reimplementing contradiction or decay engines. _(anchor: F5:L1-L7, L9-L12; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-002** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=238) `decay_tick` already advances trust decay but emits no cross-subsystem signal. _(anchor: F5:L13-L15; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-003** `[E/S]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=239) `spawn_decay_scheduler` has an optional tick observer but is asserted live only in comments/tests; the production path is the inline post-reflect `decay_tick` in `live_cli.rs`. _(anchor: F5:L15, L227; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-004** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=240) `audit_conflicts` is the existing contradiction closer and must be reused. _(anchor: F5:L16, L26; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-005** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=241) `dkg_nodes.concept_stale` defaults to and is inserted as `1`, with no real alternate population yet. _(anchor: F5:L17, L31; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-006** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=242) Phase 1 inventories every DKG SELECT/read and records whether it reads or assumes `concept_stale=1` and whether ranking is affected. _(anchor: F5:L52-L55; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-007** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=243) Before any population change, a contract fixture covers states 0, 1, and 2; state 0 must remain included and state 2 must demote rather than disappear. _(anchor: F5:L56, L155, L180; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-008** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=244) Phase 2 adds one `SsbActionType::StalenessQuarantineBroadcast`. _(anchor: F5:L58-L60; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-009** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=245) The broadcast payload carries fact ID, staleness ratio, dependent-demotion count, Cosmos-flag count, tick time, and broadcast ID. _(anchor: F5:L60; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-010** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=246) `broadcast_id` is a BLAKE3 prefix of `fact_id _(anchor:  | tick_ms`. | F5:L60; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-011** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=247) The broadcast is a single action shape with SQL parse/check, payload round-trip, golden lock, and baseline-plus-one schema bump. _(anchor: F5:L60, L158; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-012** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=248) `collect_quarantined` reuses existing decay math and selects facts with `staleness_ratio > 2.0` on the tick. _(anchor: F5:L62; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-013** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=249) `QuarantineRecord` contains fact ID and staleness ratio. _(anchor: F5:L62; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-014** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=250) Contradiction-driven quarantine uses the existing `audit_conflicts` verdict instead of a second detector. _(anchor: F5:L62, L74; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-015** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=251) Broadcast emission is attached to both the live inline tick and the scheduler observer; the observer cannot be the sole caller. _(anchor: F5:L62, L169, L227; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-016** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=252) `apply_staleness_broadcast` locates DKG concepts citing a quarantined fact, sets them to `concept_stale=2`, and returns a demotion count. _(anchor: F5:L64; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-017** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=253) DKG contradiction edges are closed bi-temporally/non-destructively when the broadcast is applied. _(anchor: F5:L64, L183; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-018** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=254) Pending broadcasts are drained after `ensure_dkg_or_rebuild` through the existing knowledge SSB sink. _(anchor: F5:L64, L93-L100; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-019** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=255) `flag_on_staleness_broadcast` transitions cited Cosmos artifacts through the existing `CosmosStatus::Stale` path and returns a count. _(anchor: F5:L66; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-020** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=256) No new Cosmos status or decay engine is introduced. _(anchor: F5:L28, L66; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-021** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=257) `QuarantineBroadcastQueue` is an in-memory `tokio::sync::mpsc` with named capacity `QUARANTINE_QUEUE_CAPACITY=256`. _(anchor: F5:L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-022** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=258) When full, the queue drops the oldest item and places a visible `dropped_count` on the next broadcast; silent loss is forbidden. _(anchor: F5:L68, L108, L157; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-023** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=259) The cascade is enabled by default; `ELAI_STALENESS_CASCADE_KILL=1` is disable-only. _(anchor: F5:L74, L103-L109, L153; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-024** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=260) Quarantine decisions are deterministic and cannot be overridden by an LLM/prompt. _(anchor: F5:L107; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-025** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=261) `get_staleness_quarantine` returns typed broadcast rows and aggregate DKG/Cosmos counts to a knowledge/staleness drawer. _(anchor: F5:L70, L86, L110; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-026** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=262) The Tauri command is registered in the invoke handler and the drawer invokes it through `@tauri-apps/api/core`. _(anchor: F5:L70; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-027** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=263) `elai dkg staleness-tick --workspace .` forces one production decay tick, drains the queue, applies both consumers, and prints a summary. _(anchor: F5:L72, L112-L114; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-028** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=264) The live trigger must journal `staleness_quarantine_broadcast` and either write `F-5-staleness-tick-*.json` or print `dependents_demoted`, with at least one DKG row changed to 2. _(anchor: F5:L111-L114, L152-L154; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-029** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=265) Mock-clock acceptance requires every citing concept to flip within one tick and the next grounding query to miss cache. _(anchor: F5:L156, L181; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-030** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=266) Queue-overflow acceptance requires visible dropped count on the next broadcast. _(anchor: F5:L157, L182; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-031** `[E/G]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=267) Completion requires executed wiring check, default-on proof, dashboard observation, golden/schema checks, cross-language parity, workspace compile, and auditor pass. _(anchor: F5:L150-L162; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-032** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=268) F-5 is fully deterministic/headless and has no RunPod/live-model split. _(anchor: F5:L162, L171, L229; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-033** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=269) The cascade uses SQLite updates and an in-process bounded queue; no network, subprocess, or external dependency is added. _(anchor: F5:L161, L176, L229; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-034** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=270) Rules-crate 90-day decay is an explicitly excluded third isolated decay in v1. _(anchor: F5:L30; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-035** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=271) Phase 2 is blocked until B-30 stabilizes `dkg_nodes`; Phase 1 can proceed independently. _(anchor: F5:L5, L27, L225; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-036** `[E/R]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=272) The stated `>1.0` flag and `>2.0` quarantine thresholds originate in SHARD; adopting `>2.0` for ELAI remains a decision to confirm. _(anchor: F5:L11, L220; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-037** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=273) Grounding-cache invalidation is only an assumed measurable until Phase 1 proves cache keys consume freshness. _(anchor: F5:L220; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-038** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=274) Drop-oldest may discard the most stale/important quarantine; visible loss and later capacity tuning are the only stated mitigations. _(anchor: F5:L226; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-039** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=275) The existing Cosmos status must be verified to contain a usable `Stale` transition before implementation. _(anchor: F5:L228; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-040** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=276) F-5’s decision record must preserve that ELAI already has bi-temporal memory semantics and that SHARD supplies a blueprint, not adopted code. _(anchor: F5:L74, L213-L217; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-041** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=277) Future composition may demote skills that cite quarantined facts and feed F-7 utility, but neither is part of F-5. _(anchor: F5:L231-L233; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F5-042** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-5`, ledger-line=278) The plan asserts each broadcast row flows through the OTLP exporter for off-machine archival. _(anchor: F5:L233; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

### Unit `F-6` (51 feature records)

- **F-002/DF002-F6-001** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=286) A learned skill is acquired only through a critic gate, governed by a separate execution-autonomy ladder, retrieved through the existing reranker, and demoted on staleness. _(anchor: F6:L1-L9, L38-L49; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-002** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=287) Delivery is ordered C6a→C6b→C6c, each gated on its predecessor. _(anchor: F6:L11-L14, L125, L187, L216; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-003** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=288) `TemplateCrystallizer` is asserted shipped and crystallizes after `CRYSTALLIZATION_THRESHOLD_N` consecutive successes. _(anchor: F6:L16-L18, L68-L74; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

- **F-002/DF002-F6-005** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=290) Existing memory `TrustTier::{T0UserDirect,T1CodebaseDerived,T2WebSourced,T3ModelEmitted}` represents provenance, not execution autonomy. _(anchor: F6:L55-L57, L85-L88; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-006** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=291) `SkillTrustTier::{S1Candidate,S2Probationary,S3Established,S4Trusted}` uses distinct `S1`–`S4` wire tags. _(anchor: F6:L127-L131; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-007** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=292) No `From`/`Into` conversion may exist between skill and memory trust types. _(anchor: F6:L130-L131, L291; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-008** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=293) `LearnedSkill` stores UUID, concept class, plan-skeleton hash, tier, invocation/failure counts, acquired/last-verified times, optional embedding reference, and optional critic-verdict SSB ID. _(anchor: F6:L132-L136; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-009** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=294) Promotion requires named per-tier minimum invocation, maximum failure-rate, and minimum-age thresholds. _(anchor: F6:L137-L142; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-010** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=295) Promotion caps at S3 unless explicit human promotion permission is present; S4 promotion is human-gated. _(anchor: F6:L139-L142; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-011** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=296) `permits_autonomous()` is true only for `S4Trusted`; every sub-S4 skill routes to the human gate. _(anchor: F6:L143-L144, L161-L170; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-012** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=297) The autonomy decision conjoins existing `should_auto_advance(...)` with the skill-tier permission. _(anchor: F6:L161-L168; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-013** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=298) Sub-S4 denial reuses the existing auto-advance-denied observability rather than creating a new skill event. _(anchor: F6:L167-L169; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-014** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=299) The autonomy barrier is default-on; `ELAI_SKILL_AUTONOMY_BARRIER_DISABLE=1` is the only allowed disable switch. _(anchor: F6:L169-L170, L285-L287; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-015** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=300) A terminal default-off `ELAI_SKILL_LIBRARY_ENABLE` flag is forbidden. _(anchor: F6:L285-L287, L325-L327; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

- **F-002/DF002-F6-028** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=313) `acquire_learned_skill` is a separately testable async method and makes zero new LLM/critic calls. _(anchor: F6:L194-L200; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

- **F-002/DF002-F6-031** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=316) `description_embedding_ref` is populated during acquisition; no new model or dependency is introduced. _(anchor: F6:L202-L208; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-032** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=317) Retrieval recall A/B is measurement-only Tier-2 and never gates execution or completion. _(anchor: F6:L207-L208, L406-L410; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F6-033** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-6`, ledger-line=318) Staleness is `(now_secs-last_verified_secs)/SKILL_EXPECTED_LIFETIME_SECS` using a named lifetime constant. _(anchor: F6:L218-L221; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_

### Unit `F-7` (48 feature records)

- **F-002/DF002-F7-001** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=344) F-7 contains two independently shippable halves: CBR ranking ships first; immutable-rule lattice proof is an E-53 amendment. _(anchor: F7:L1-L13, L52-L82; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-002** `[E/S]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=345) Existing ranking is raw `first_try_success_rate=successes/bucket_len`, allowing a 1/1 bucket to outrank 95/100. _(anchor: F7:L17-L21; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-003** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=346) `laplace_utility(outcomes,block_count,alpha,beta)` starts from `(successes+alpha)/(n+alpha+beta)`. _(anchor: F7:L54-L56; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-004** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=347) Utility is down-weighted by `block_ratio=block_count/max(n,1)` and clamped to `[0,1]`. _(anchor: F7:L56; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-005** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=348) Default priors are named constants `LAPLACE_ALPHA=1.0` and `LAPLACE_BETA=1.0`; zero total prior is forbidden. _(anchor: F7:L56; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-006** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=349) `TemplateUtility` stores concept class, plan-skeleton hash, utility, old first-try ratio, block ratio, coverage, and sample count. _(anchor: F7:L56; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-007** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=350) When multiple templates match identity, `CompiledExecutor::try_execute` uses utility instead of raw first-try rate for selection/tie-break. _(anchor: F7:L58; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-008** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=351) `first_try_success_rate` remains stored and displayed as the measurement baseline. _(anchor: F7:L58, L82; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-009** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=352) Every ranking decision emits `template_utility_ranked`. _(anchor: F7:L58-L60; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-010** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=353) `TemplateUtilityRankedPayload` carries all utility inputs plus chosen pipeline ID. _(anchor: F7:L60; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-011** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=354) `TemplateUtilityRanked` has SQL/check/parse/round-trip/golden registration and a baseline-plus-one schema impact when shipped alone. _(anchor: F7:L60; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-012** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=355) `elai template-ranking list _(anchor: show --concept-class X [--json]` exposes buckets sorted descending by utility. | F7:L62; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-013** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=356) Half-B’s reconciled UI is `TemplateRankingDrawer.svelte` plus `commands_f7.rs::get_template_ranking`. _(anchor: F7:L86-L96, L121-L124, L136-L137; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-014** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=357) The surface renders utility and first-try ratio side by side. _(anchor: F7:L64, L143; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-015** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=358) Half-B’s live trigger is `template-ranking list --json`, producing one ranking row and JSON with utility plus sibling first-try ratio. _(anchor: F7:L138-L143, L185-L190; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-016** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=359) Smoothing correctness requires 95/100 to outrank 1/1 in unit and live ordering. _(anchor: F7:L189, L199; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-017** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=360) Empirical-Bayes per-class priors are deferred until at least 100 sessions exist. _(anchor: F7:L239; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-018** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=361) CBR uplift is measured only after at least 100 labelled sessions against the old baseline; no numeric uplift gates this F-N. _(anchor: F7:L23, L82, L190, L238; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-019** `[E/D]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=362) Springdrift’s 7,056-pair/100%-coverage and +10.7% results are synthetic/unreproduced hypotheses, not ELAI targets. _(anchor: F7:L23, L228-L230; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-020** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=363) Half A enumerates the `mutable=0` security and finance rule subset as a set. _(anchor: F7:L17-L20, L66-L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-021** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=364) Its SMT obligation asserts coverage of every declared class by at least one immutable rule. _(anchor: F7:L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-022** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=365) Its SMT obligation asserts that rule-set union never reduces the blocked-action set. _(anchor: F7:L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-023** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=366) Its SMT obligation asserts that no two immutable rules contradict on the same argv/class. _(anchor: F7:L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-024** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=367) `SmtLib2Obligation` stores class count, rule count, SMT-LIB2 text, and a BLAKE3 obligation hash. _(anchor: F7:L68; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-025** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=368) Lattice DB access propagates `rusqlite::Error` and production code has no `.unwrap()`/`.expect()`. _(anchor: F7:L68, L194; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-026** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=369) Half A is blocked until E-53’s `VerifierKind::Z3Smt` route stops returning the `z3_stub` inapplicable outcome. _(anchor: F7:L7-L9, L19-L20; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-027** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=370) The proposed Z3 dispatch accepts a lattice discriminator/constructor and calls the shared formal-verification `run` pipeline. _(anchor: F7:L70; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-028** `[E/R]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=371) If Z3 is absent, the route returns `RunpodPending`, never a fabricated `Verified`. _(anchor: F7:L70, L134; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-029** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=372) With Z3 present, discharge yields `Verified`; a counterexample yields `Refuted{counterexample_label}`. _(anchor: F7:L70; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-030** `[E/P]` `SURFACE` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=373) The original proposed CLI spelling is `elai formal-verify lattice`, which reports verifier/outcome/hash/class/rule counts. _(anchor: F7:L72; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-031** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=374) Proposed `safety_lattice_verified` carries class/rule counts, obligation hash, outcome string, verified Boolean, and RunPod-pending Boolean. _(anchor: F7:L72-L74; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-032** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=375) Local/no-Z3 execution would journal `verified=false,runpod_pending=true`. _(anchor: F7:L72; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-033** `[E/P]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=376) The deterministic Half-A fixture uses a seeded in-memory rules DB and exercises the real formal-verification dispatcher up to the Z3 boundary. _(anchor: F7:L76; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-034** `[E/G]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=377) A complete fixture must produce a well-formed obligation and local `RunpodPending` with hash; an incomplete fixture must encode its uncovered-class assertion. _(anchor: F7:L76, L191; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
- **F-002/DF002-F7-035** `[E/D]` (unit=`F-7`, ledger-line=378) The actual Z3 discharge is a separate ignored/live checkbox; a complete set must verify and a planted contradiction must refute. _(anchor: F7:L76, L192; source: DEEP-F-002.discovery.md)_
